| Front/templates/admin/classesperbranch.php | Front/templates/admin/js/classesperbranch.js | /classesperbranch.php, /branches/[id]/classes | partial - branch-scoped class listing, filters, bridge, and locked branch table restored; visual audit remains | t_branch, t_class, auto, themeforest | brid, ids, name, lname, language, dob, maxStudents, from, to, order_date_from, order_date_to, q | Restored the legacy branch-scoped Classes Listing: `/classesperbranch.php?brid=` resolves raw numeric, UUID, and encrypted legacy branch ids; missing `brid` falls back to `/classes`; supported legacy filter aliases are preserved into the modern branch route; the page shows `Classes Management For Branch: {branch}`, `{branch} Branch Classes Listing`, New Class, checkbox select-all, S.N., Image, Class, Language, Max Students, locked Branch column, Date, Action, per-column filters, created-date range, Clear, 10/20/50/100/150/All page sizes, Excel/CSV export, print, legacy image fallback, update dialog, class dashboard action, and legacy-style soft deactivate. Remaining work is exact Metronic/DataTables copy/PDF/XLS/print visual parity, lightbox image-preview behavior, permission-gated action visibility from legacy `Check::protectPageOrFunction`, and final logged-in browser smoke. |
| Front/templates/admin/dailyreport.php | Front/templates/admin/js/dailyreport.js | /dailyreport.php, /daily-reports/[id]/edit, /daily-reports/new?childId= | partial - legacy daily report edit/create bridge restored; form field audit remains | auto, themeforest | date, fid, id, isdraft | The main and draft listings now link into the modern edit route with legacy child/report context preserved through migrated IDs and report dates. `/dailyreport.php?fid=` now resolves raw numeric, UUID, and encrypted legacy daily-report ids into the modern edit form; `/dailyreport.php?id=` resolves raw numeric, UUID, legacy key, and encrypted child ids into a preselected new daily report and preserves `date=` as the modern report date. Remaining work is exact `dailyreport.php` form layout/field validation/progress behavior, including the full food/sleep/health/clothes/attachments workflow audit against `dailyreport.js`. |
| Front/templates/admin/dailyreports.php | Front/templates/admin/js/dailyreports.js | /dailyreports.php, /daily-reports | partial - legacy daily report roster, filters, export, page sizes, submitted scope, and bridge restored; visual audit remains | auto, themeforest |  | Restored the legacy Daily Reports Listing as the default submitted/non-draft table view with Child #/Image/F Name/L Name/Status/Branch/Class/Report Date/Date/Action columns, migrated `images/EmpPhoto` filename fallback, global search, branch/class/workflow filters, legacy column filters, created-date range, 10/20/50/100/150/All page sizes, Excel/CSV export, print action, New Daily Report, view/edit/print/delete actions, and legacy present/absent/holiday status badges from preserved `t_daily_report.status`. `/dailyreports.php` now redirects to the modern submitted daily reports roster. Remaining work is exact TableTools copy/PDF/XLS/print visual parity and final Browser visual smoke. |
| Front/templates/admin/dailyreportsd.php | Front/templates/admin/js/dailyreportsd.js | /dailyreportsd.php, /daily-reports/drafts | partial - legacy draft roster, filters, export, page sizes, selected approval, and bridge restored; visual audit remains | auto, themeforest |  | Restored `/daily-reports/drafts` as a real Draft Reports Listing instead of a redirect, with the same legacy Child #/Image/F Name/L Name/Status/Branch/Class/Report Date/Date/Action columns, filters, date range, page sizes, export, print, and migrated photo fallback. Added selected-row approval backed by `approveDailyReports`, preserving the legacy guard that present drafts with undefined breakfast/lunch portions are skipped. `/dailyreportsd.php` now redirects to the modern draft daily reports roster. Remaining work is direct-approval setting parity and exact TableTools visual/PDF export parity. |
| Front/templates/admin/doctors.php | Front/templates/admin/js/doctors.js | /doctors.php, /employees/doctors | partial - legacy doctors roster columns, q bridge, export/print, page sizes, and selected deactivate restored; form modal audit remains | auto, themeforest | q | Restored the shared staff roster shape for doctors with legacy `#`, Image, F Name, L Name, DOB, Branch, Mobile, Nationality, Gender, Date, Status, and Action columns; 10/20/50/100/150/All page sizes; Excel/CSV export in legacy column order; print action; selected-row deactivation backed by the org-safe employee action; and `/doctors.php?q=` redirect hydration into the modern roster search across row values. Remaining work is exact TableTools copy/PDF visual parity and the legacy selected-employee form modal behavior from `doctors.js`. |
| Front/templates/admin/exportdb.php |  | /exportdb.php, /settings/export | partial - legacy export bridge restored; field/action audit remains |  |  | `/exportdb.php` now redirects to the modern settings export page. Remaining work is exact field/action audit against legacy export behavior. |
| Front/templates/admin/food.php | Front/templates/admin/js/food.js | /food.php, /food | partial - legacy food listing roster restored; delete/backfill audit remains | Food, auto, themeforest |  | `/food.php` now redirects to the modern food listing. The modern page now restores the legacy Food Types/Food Listing surface with `#`, Type, Name, Active, Date, and Action columns; BreakFast/Lunch/Dessert/Snack category labels; On/Off status wording; create/update Food Type modal fields for Food Type, Food, and Status; per-column filters plus Clear; 10/20/50/100/150/All page sizes; Excel/CSV export; print action; and newest-first default ordering approximating legacy `fid desc`. Remaining work is exact legacy `fid` preservation/backfill for the `#` column, TableTools copy/PDF visual parity, and delete semantics audit because legacy soft-deleted `t_food.deleted` while the modern schema currently has active/inactive plus hard delete. |
| Front/templates/admin/food_calendar.php | Front/templates/admin/js/food_calendar.js | /food_calendar.php, /food/calendar | partial - legacy food calendar bridge, query state, print handoff, and all-branch apply restored; meal/action audit remains | BreakFast, auto, themeforest | id | `/food_calendar.php?id=`/`brid=` now resolves raw numeric, UUID, and encrypted legacy branch ids into `/food/calendar?branch=...`, the modern page initializes and keeps branch/month/year query state as the user navigates, the Print action opens the visible branch/month/year like legacy `food_calendar.js`, and blank-day saves can apply Breakfast/Lunch/Snack/Dessert to This Branch or All Branches. Remaining work is exact FullCalendar visual behavior, legacy Early Dinner schema support, and permission-gated action visibility parity. |
| Front/templates/admin/forbidden.php |  | /forbidden.php | restored - legacy privilege-denied screen restored |  |  | `/forbidden.php` now renders the legacy standalone dark Forbidden screen with the original privilege warning copy. |
| Front/templates/admin/getmap.php |  | /getmap.php | restored - legacy geocode POST helper restored with secure Google Maps configuration |  | address, latitude, longitude, submit_address, submit_coordinates | `/getmap.php` now accepts the legacy form POST fields for address geocoding and coordinate reverse-geocoding, preserves the legacy text responses for form callers, supports JSON callers, and replaces the old unauthenticated HTTP Google calls with HTTPS plus server-side `GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`/`GOOGLE_GEOCODING_API_KEY`. |
| Front/templates/admin/holiday_calendar.php | Front/templates/admin/js/holiday_calendar.js | /holiday_calendar.php, /settings/holidays | partial - legacy holiday calendar bridge, modal semantics, and multi-day reminders restored; notification delivery audit remains | Holiday, auto, themeforest |  | `/holiday_calendar.php` now redirects to the modern holidays calendar. The modern create/update modal now mirrors legacy `Create Holiday`/`Update Holiday` copy, primary `Description` field, default Repeated recurrence, Date label, `Notifications` section, legacy notification subject/message placeholders, 1-7 day labels, Create/Update buttons, apostrophe rejection for Description/message, and persisted legacy `daysbefore[]` multi-select reminders via `Holiday.notificationDaysBefore` with `daysBefore` fallback. Remaining work is exact FullCalendar visual behavior, employee immediate strike notification delivery, and permission-gated action visibility parity. |
